We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: https://hng0303.github.io/VietFashion.

</details>


### [103] [OmniDirector: General Multi-Shot Camera Cloning without Cross-Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13432)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Zhixue Fang,Xiaohan Li,Yan Zhou,Zijie Meng,Zhimin Zhang,Yawen Luo,Guoxin Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDirector框架，通过将相机参数编码为网格运动视频（camera grid）作为统一表征，并构建百万级相机网格-视频对进行训练，结合分层提示扩展代理，实现了对多模态扩散变换器的导演级控制，在复杂相机运动克隆任务中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用参数化表示无法处理多镜头生成，要么因合成跨配对数据存在数据稀缺问题，导致复杂相机运动克隆效果不佳。

Method: 1) 提出通用相机运动表征camera grid，将相机参数编码为网格运动视频；2) 提出OmniDirector统一框架，在百万级camera grid-视频对上训练；3) 设计分层提示扩展代理，通过理解信号关系系统描述相机运动和视觉内容，协调不同控制信号。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架具有优越性能和出色的可控性。

Conclusion: OmniDirector实现了对多模态扩散变换器的导演级控制，有效解决了复杂相机运动克隆问题。

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [104] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian,Yuehan Xian,Jing Liang,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISA方法，利用离线VLM对占据世界模型进行语义审计，通过可靠性加权的分类法、属性因子和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，提升了nuScenes数据集上的mIoU性能，证明了VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器而非通用字幕嵌入目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义3D占据模型在物体和罕见类别上存在错误影响自由空间解释和碰撞检测；常用的将3D体素或物体特征与裁剪字幕嵌入对齐的VLM策略虽能提升文本空间相似度，但未能可靠提升闭集占据mIoU。

Method: VISA在训练时对每个物理物体实例的代表性裁剪向离线VLM查询，获得包含类假设、混淆、可靠性、属性和证据的结构化审计，沿物体轨迹传播；审计被锚定到匹配的3D物体体素，通过可靠性加权分类法、属性因子和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，推理时不需VLM。

Result: 在nuScenes上，OccWorld的mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，GaussianWorld从21.36提升至21.91；GaussianWorld上物体mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，罕见类mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。

Conclusion: VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器来提升闭集占据模型的性能，而非作为通用字幕嵌入目标。

Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

</details>


### [105] [Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13488)
*Siyu Zhou,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAPR-Net，一种结合卷积与Transformer的粗到细点云配准框架，通过跨注意力机制融合局部与全局信息，并引入变换不变性几何特征，在四个骨骼数据集上实现94.2%的配准召回率、1.992mm的RMSE，为计算机辅助手术提供高精度配准基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分到完整点云配准因重叠率变化、点密度波动和噪声存在而极具挑战；现有Transformer方法仅用于全局上下文聚合，忽略了对于精确对应至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。

Method: 提出GAPR-Net，采用粗到细架构结合卷积与Transformer模块，通过跨注意力机制融合部分点云与完整点云之间的局部与全局信息；设计变换不变的点向几何特征表示，鲁棒地捕捉每个点相对于其邻域的几何特征。

Result: 在胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸肋软骨四个几何结构不同的骨骼数据集上，整体配准召回率达94.2%，RMSE为1.992mm，旋转和平移的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了部分到完整点云配准问题，实现了高精度3D点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中精确的手术导航和机器人干预提供了关键基础。

Abstract: Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.

</details>


### [106] [Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13496)
*Mingkun Lei,Tong Zhao,Liangyu Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudCache提出了一种预算驱动的扩散模型步骤级缓存方法，通过离线搜索（模拟退火+爬山算法）在固定计算预算下优化缓存策略，并引入缓存感知的时间调度对齐，在同等推理预算下比启发式缓存基线获得更好的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于阈值的启发式步骤级缓存方法无法直接优化最终输出质量，且推理延迟随输入变化、难以在部署时控制，因此需要一种能在固定计算预算下直接优化输出质量的缓存策略。

Method: 1) 将问题转化为在固定预算下搜索最优缓存策略的组合优化问题；2) 结合模拟退火与确定性爬山算法进行离线搜索，分钟级内找到高质量缓存策略；3) 在预算极紧时引入缓存感知的调度对齐，调整时间离散化以适应所选缓存策略，减少缓存导致的轨迹不匹配。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上，BudCache在相同推理预算下实现了比启发式缓存基线更好的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过将缓存决策从阈值驱动转为预算驱动，BudCache实现了可控、高效且质量更优的扩散模型加速，且离线搜索策略无在线开销。

Abstract: Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache

</details>


### [107] [Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13503)
*Judith Vilella-Cantos,Juan José Cabrera,Mónica Ballesta,David Valiente,Luis Payá*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种融合异质LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）并在推理时采用学习型重排序策略的地点识别方法，在葡萄园等非结构化重复场景下，Recall@1指标相比单传感器方法提升20%，结合重排序提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境（如农业田地）中的鲁棒定位是自主系统的关键挑战，而重复场景（如葡萄园）中地点识别的真阳性查找尤为困难。

Method: 提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法：1）早期融合两个异质LiDAR传感器（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）的数据；2）在推理时引入学习型重排序策略，以提升重复场景中的识别性能。

Result: 在TEMPO-VINE数据集上，Recall@1指标相比单传感器方法提升20%，结合重排序提升30%，且优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 多传感器异质LiDAR数据融合与推理时重排序策略能显著提升非结构化重复环境下的地点识别性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.

</details>


### [108] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测量校准的多摄像头融合方法，通过对单摄像头定位误差（单应性校准、人体检测、运动跟踪）进行组件级显式量化，来优化多摄像头数据融合。实验表明，该方法在绝对精度上提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内视觉定位系统中的多摄像头数据融合通常被视为黑箱组件，仅通过端到端评估，掩盖了其内部机制贡献。本文旨在探究显式刻画单摄像头定位误差能否用于校准和优化多摄像头数据融合。

Method: 提出测量校准的融合方法，集成组件级误差量化，分别隔离单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪三个环节的误差，并进行组件级评估以量化各环节误差贡献。

Result: 数据融合相比单摄像头基线提升了定位精度；测量校准融合在绝对精度上相比标准融合提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性。

Conclusion: 显式误差表征对于设计视觉室内定位系统的数据融合策略具有重要价值，特别是在需要稳定、连续运动估计的应用场景中。

Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

</details>


### [109] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu,Haitao Lin,Jingbo Zhang,Wenyao Zhang,Chenghao Gu,Heng Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskWAM通过统一掩码Transformer架构，将物体掩码同时作为输入和预测目标，解决了世界动作模型在视觉推理中的空间模糊性和语义噪声问题，在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）存在两大瓶颈：(1) 标准文本输入在杂乱场景中会产生指代模糊；(2) 非结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定，且受任务无关背景干扰。亟需一种能提供精确空间引导且具有语义鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出MaskWAM，采用统一的混合Transformer（MoT）架构，将掩码同时作为显式输入（如目标物体掩码作为首帧视觉提示）和预测目标（预测未来掩码提供物体中心语义监督），实现物体中心的世界动作建模。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上，MaskWAM在语言清晰和语言模糊两种任务设置下均显著超越基线方法。

Conclusion: 掩码条件化作为视觉驱动架构的原生输入，相比文本能提供更强的空间引导，建立起精确且鲁棒的泛化范式；掩码预测作为语义监督可有效抑制视觉噪声，甚至能增强标准的文本条件化WAM。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

</details>


### [110] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PCA和LDA的降维显著性图生成方法，无需人工标注或领域知识即可从原始训练数据中获取显著性信息，并在虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD等五个生物特征攻击检测领域验证了其有效性，性能超越基线甚至部分SOTA方法，解决了显著性引导训练的可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性获取方法（如人工标注）成本高、领域特异性强、可扩展性有限，限制了显著性引导训练在生物特征展示攻击检测（PAD）中的广泛采用。本文旨在克服这一障碍，提出低成本、高可扩展性的显著性获取方案。

Method: 利用经典降维技术PCA（主成分分析）和LDA（线性判别分析）从原始训练数据中直接生成显著性图，无需人类标注或领域知识。在三个已探索显著性领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）和两个新颖领域（指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）上进行验证。

Result: 使用降维显著性图训练的模型在所有测试领域均超过基线方法，部分场景达到甚至超越现有SOTA显著性方法，且无需任何资源投入或领域特定工具。

Conclusion: 本文提出的降维显著性图方法以零额外成本解决了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测中的可扩展性瓶颈，为该范式在更广泛领域的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

</details>


### [111] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitResEdit是一种针对比特级残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑方法，通过结合比特级指导（BitEdit）和残差代码组合（ResEdit），在PIE-Bench上实现了优于现有方法的文本对齐和背景保持效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR编辑器主要操作token流、特征或扁平的下一个token logits，未充分利用比特级残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头和加法多尺度残差代码场。作者意在利用这些被忽略的结构设计更优的编辑方法。

Method: 提出BitResEdit，包含两个组件：1) BitEdit：通过源-目标对比倾斜后CFG的逐比特log-odds，并进行伯努利KL信任域投影实现源负向引导；2) ResEdit：将采样比特转换为逐尺度连续代码残差，用法化掩码门控后通过生成器的原生求和尺度机制重新注入。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B，BitResEdit在相同骨干的VAR编辑器中实现了最强的文本对齐，编辑区域的CLIP分数相比最强先验编辑器提升+1.07，同时背景保持与其相当。

Conclusion: BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保持方面发挥互补作用，证明了比特级指导与代码组合耦合的有效性。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>


### [112] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比信息增强和领域对抗训练能否提升E2E-VarNet网络从成人到新生儿的MR图像重建泛化能力。实验表明，混合训练和混合对抗训练均优于仅用成人数据训练，其中混合对抗训练在R=4时取得最佳SSIM（0.924）和PSNR（33.98 dB），在R=8时SSIM最优（0.848），而混合训练的PSNR最优（29.56 dB）。t-SNE分析显示对抗训练增加了不同域数据潜在表征的重叠。结论：对比信息增强和领域对抗训练改善了深度学习MR重建的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习MR重建模型在成人数据上表现良好，但在新生儿数据上因域偏移（domain shift）导致泛化能力下降，亟需提升模型从成人到新生儿的跨域重建性能。

Method: 对比三种训练策略：（1）仅用未增强成人数据训练（Unaug-Only）；（2）混合训练：成人数据+新生儿对比信息增强的成人数据（Mixed）；（3）混合训练+领域对抗目标（Mixed-DAT）。在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，在新生儿和成人测试数据上以R=4和R=8加速因子评估，并进行t-SNE特征分析。

Result: Mixed和Mixed-DAT在新生儿数据上均优于Unaug-Only。R=4时Mixed-DAT最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027, PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）；R=8时Mixed-DAT的SSIM最优（0.848±0.031），Mixed的PSNR最优（29.56±0.83 dB）。t-SNE显示Mixed-DAT增加了不同域数据潜在表征的重叠。

Conclusion: 对比信息增强和领域对抗训练有效改善了深度学习MR重建从成人到新生儿的泛化性能，提示该方法可提升欠采样新生儿MR重建对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

</details>


### [113] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvTexture++，首个专门面向视频超分辨率中纹理增强的事件驱动框架，通过利用事件信号的高频时空细节渐进式恢复纹理，并引入时间纹理对齐模块解决大运动导致的纹理闪烁问题，可作为即插即用模块提升现有 VSR 模型性能，在五个数据集上达到 SOTA，最高提升 1.55 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动 VSR 工作主要将事件信号用于运动细化和光流估计，忽略了事件在纹理增强方面的潜力；同时大运动场景下纹理区域的时间一致性难以保证，易出现纹理闪烁问题。

Method: 提出 EvTexture++ 框架，包含：(1) 定制化纹理增强分支；(2) 迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息渐进式恢复纹理；(3) 时间纹理对齐模块，利用事件连续时间运动线索估计纹理感知光流，实现帧间纹理精确对齐；整体设计为即插即用模块。

Result: 在五个数据集上达到 SOTA 性能；集成到现有 VSR 模型后在纹理丰富的 Vid4 数据集上最高提升 1.55 dB PSNR。

Conclusion: 事件信号在 VSR 中不仅可用于运动细化，更能有效用于纹理增强；所提框架兼具纹理恢复精度与时间一致性，且具有良好的泛化性和即插即用能力。

Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种级联式空间-频谱双域网络，结合辅助特征增强模块，用于城市垃圾的高效自动分割，在三个公共数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市扩张和人口增长导致垃圾产量激增，现有深度学习方法虽在垃圾分割中表现良好，但依赖大型骨干网络导致效率低下，且在杂乱场景中性能退化严重。

Method: 提出了级联式双域分割网络：空间域捕获局部结构依赖，频谱域提取全局上下文关系；此外设计了辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），增强目标物体边界和斑点放大以改善杂乱场景分割。

Result: 在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste三个数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过级联双域设计和AFEM模块，在保持效率的同时提升了垃圾分割性能，尤其适用于杂乱场景下的自动垃圾回收系统。

Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [115] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对监控场景中车辆颜色识别面临的高度类别不平衡问题，通过合成少数类数据、结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合等方法，在UFPR-VeSV数据集上实现94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率相比近期文献提升8.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实监控系统中车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，导致整体准确率无法有效评估稀有但操作上重要的颜色类别性能，亟需针对严重类别不平衡条件下的车辆颜色识别进行系统研究。

Method: 采用两种现成生成策略进行少数类合成：RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑；结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合。

Result: 最佳方法在UFPR-VeSV数据集上达到94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率比近期文献提升8.2个百分点。人工错误分析显示多数剩余错误对人类标注者而言也存在视觉模糊性。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强策略有效缓解了类别不平衡问题，显著提升了车辆颜色识别性能；同时揭示了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在实际局限性。

Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

</details>


### [116] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo将可变数量的未定位RGB视图压缩为K个潜在令牌（一个全局状态），并通过流匹配将噪声点独立传输到表面来解码定向3D表面点，实现任意分辨率解码，同时通过推理时的光度梯度引导抑制局部不一致性。该方法在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型存在两方面局限：逐视图方法输出重叠且未对齐的点图，输出量随输入数量线性增长；全局潜在方法则局限于固定的低分辨率输出。两者都无法将多视图的几何不变性转化为高效、可扩展的3D表示。

Method: 1) 将可变数量未定位RGB视图压缩为K个潜在令牌，形成全局状态；2) 通过流匹配（flow matching）将噪声点独立传输到3D表面，实现从数千到数百万点的任意分辨率解码；3) 在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度作为推理时引导项，抑制独立逐点解码固有的局部不一致性。

Result: 1) 在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线方法；2) 比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级；3) 是唯一结合全局潜在与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo通过全局潜在表示与流匹配解码的结合，成功打破了前馈3D重建中输出分辨率的固定限制，同时保持高质量的表面重建和高效推理，为可扩展的3D重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

</details>


### [117] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialClaw是一个无需训练的框架，采用代码作为动作接口，通过状态化Python内核逐步执行空间推理，在20个3D/4D基准上平均准确率59.9%，超越现有方法11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理智能体要么采用单次代码执行（无法根据中间结果调整策略），要么使用结构化工具调用接口（灵活性不足），难以应对开放式的复杂3D/4D空间推理任务。本文旨在设计更灵活的动作接口以提升VLM的空间推理能力。

Method: 提出SpatialClaw框架，采用代码作为动作接口，维护一个状态化Python内核，预加载输入帧及感知与几何原语库。VLM驱动的智能体每步编写一个可执行代码单元，基于所有先前输出进行条件化执行，灵活组合感知结果并适应中间文本/视觉观察及任务需求。

Result: 在20个空间推理基准上（覆盖静态和动态3D/4D任务），SpatialClaw平均准确率59.9%，超越最新空间智能体11.2个百分点，在6个不同VLM骨干上均有一致提升，无需任何基准或模型特定适配。

Conclusion: 采用代码作为动作接口、支持逐步执行和状态维护的框架能显著提升VLM在开放空间推理任务中的表现，验证了动作接口设计对工具增强型智能体能力的关键影响。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [118] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker是一个多智能体管道，通过规划器和评论家两个智能体，赋予现有图像生成器交错生成（文本-图像序列）能力。它使用SFT进行格式冷启动，并用GRPO和单步奖励（准确率奖励+逐步奖励）优化评论家的指令修正能力，在交错生成基准上达到接近Nano Banana和GPT-5的水平，同时显著提升基础模型在推理基准（如WISE和RISE）上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图像生成和编辑上表现出色，但由于架构限制无法实现交错生成（文本-图像序列），而交错生成在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作等场景中具有重要应用。即使最新的统一多模态模型在这方面表现也有限。

Method: 提出InterleaveThinker多智能体管道：(1) 规划器代理组织图像-文本输入序列，指示生成器每步执行内容；(2) 评论家代理评估生成器输出，识别偏离指令的样本并精炼指令重新生成。构建Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k和Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k进行格式冷启动，再使用Interleave-Critic-RL-13k通过GRPO和单步奖励（准确率奖励+逐步奖励）训练评论家的逐步指令修正能力。

Result: InterleaveThinker在多种图像生成器上均提升性能，在交错生成基准上达到接近Nano Banana和GPT-5的性能。同时显著提升基础模型在推理基准上的表现，例如在4-step FLUX.2-klein上，WISE和RISE均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 提出首个多智能体管道InterleaveThinker，以即插即用的方式赋予现有图像生成器交错生成能力，通过规划-批评-修正机制和单步强化学习克服了长轨迹优化难题，实验验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


### [119] [An Extensible and Lightweight Unified Architecture for Demosaicing Pixel-bin Image Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13136)
*Saurabh Kumar,Nutan Sairam Yenneti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化统一架构，用于对不同像素合并传感器的RAW数据进行去马赛克处理，并引入无需学习的CFA识别模块实现即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素合并传感器在智能手机中应用广泛，但其较大的颜色间分离度使得去马赛克具有挑战性，且现有深度学习方法仅针对特定CFA，需维护多个模型，占用资源多。

Method: 提出模块化统一架构，可扩展且轻量；引入无需学习的CFA识别模块，自动检测RAW数据的CFA类型。

Result: 在提供更高图像质量的同时，实现了对不同像素合并传感器的统一去马赛克。

Conclusion: 该统一架构能够替代多个CFA专用模型，节省资源并简化开发维护，同时保持高性能。

Abstract: Pixel-bin image sensors are becoming the default choice for smartphone cameras due to their resolution vs light-gathering trade-off. However, their larger inter-color separation compared to the Bayer color filter array (CFA) makes them challenging to demosaic. Furthermore, existing deep learning-based demosaicing methods are CFA-specific, requiring multiple individual models that take up precious onboard resources and demand larger development and maintenance efforts. In this work, we propose a modular unified architecture for demosaicing various pixel-bin sensors that provides higher image quality while being extensible and lightweight. Additionally, to enable plug-and-play operation, we introduce a learning-free CFA-identification module to detect the CFA type of raw data accurately.

</details>


### [120] [Diffusion Transformer World-Action Model for AV Scene Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12987)
*Ruslan Sharifullin,Benjamin Jiang,Kai Xi Chew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的潜空间世界模型，用于自动驾驶的未来场景预测。通过对比六种冻结编码器，发现V-JEPA2结合时间上下文使转向RMSE降低40%。训练潜空间扩散变压器（DiT）时识别出四个关键要素：空间token、x₀目标、残差锚定和匹配目标不确定性。在Stable-Diffusion-VAE编解码流程中，揭示了失真度量（余弦相似度、SSIM）偏向模糊均值而掩盖扩散模型优势的问题。扩散模型KID达0.078，是回归模型0.375的4.8倍。模型具有真实动作可控性（转向与场景位移的Spearman ρ=0.81，回归为-0.18）。提出170万参数的"跳跃"模型，恢复真实运动幅度（1.02×真实值），而单次模型不足一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要可预测未来场景的世界模型，但现有紧凑模型面临未来不确定性带来的模糊预测问题，且标准失真度量（如余弦相似度、SSIM）误导性地奖励模糊回归均值而非真实感预测。

Method: 采用紧凑潜空间世界模型架构：冻结编码器提取潜表示→潜空间扩散变压器（DiT）预测未来潜码→冻结解码器渲染256×256帧（最长8秒）。系统化诊断识别DiT四个关键成分：空间token、x₀目标、残差锚定、匹配目标不确定性的采样。提出170万参数"跳跃"模型解决运动捕捉不足问题。

Result: V-JEPA2编码器使转向RMSE降低40%（vs最佳单帧编码器）。扩散模型KID 0.078 vs 回归0.375（4.8×优势）。动作可控性：转向与场景位移Spearman ρ=0.81，回归为-0.18。"跳跃"模型恢复1.02×真实运动幅度，单次模型不足一半。

Conclusion: 潜空间扩散世界模型在紧凑规模下实现了比回归模型更真实、动作可控的未来场景预测。揭示了感知-失真前沿的权衡，并通过训练时校准实现无测试时真值的实用部署。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let an autonomous vehicle predict future camera scenes from its own planned controls, enabling planning and simulation without real-world rollouts, but at compact, trainable scale the futures are ambiguous and the field's standard distortion metrics actively mislead: they reward a blurry regression mean over a realistic prediction. We confront this with a compact latent world model that, given the present front-camera latent and a sequence of ego-actions, predicts future scene latents a frozen decoder renders to $256 \times 256$ frames up to 8 seconds ahead, evaluated on 150 held-out nuScenes scenes. We first benchmark where to predict: across six frozen encoders spanning four representation families, V-JEPA2 with temporal context reduces steering RMSE by 40% over the best single-frame encoder. We then train a latent Diffusion Transformer (DiT) and, through a controlled diagnosis, identify the four ingredients it needs: spatial tokens, the $x_0$ objective, residual anchoring, and sampling matched to target uncertainty. In a Stable-Diffusion-VAE encode-predict-decode pipeline we expose the central tension: distortion metrics (cosine similarity, SSIM) favor the blurry mean, masking that the diffusion model is far closer to the real frame distribution. Inception-based FID and KID reveal a clean perception-distortion frontier: diffusion attains KID 0.078 versus 0.375 for regression ($4.8\times$ better), and a deployable train-derived calibration makes this practical without test-time ground truth. The model is genuinely action-controllable (steering drives scene displacement, Spearman $ρ= 0.81$, vs $-0.18$ for regression). We trace limited single-pass motion to a shared-present anchor and engineer a compact 1.7M-parameter "jump" model that recovers full ground-truth motion magnitude ($1.02\times$ GT), where single-pass models capture less than half.

</details>


### [121] [Cascade Classification of Dermoscopic Images of Skin Neoplasms with Controllable Sensitivity and External Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13135)
*Elena S. Kozachok,Sergey S. Seregin,Aleksandr V. Kozachok,Ilya P. Latyshev,Oleg I. Samovarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种深度学习架构（ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S）在皮肤镜图像分类中的表现，探索了三种分类方案（二分类、单阶段四分类、两阶段级联分类）。模型在国际公开数据集（ISIC Archive）上训练，在俄罗斯临床数据集（Melanoscope AI、Sechenov University）上验证。结果显示：二阶段内部ROC-AUC达0.952-0.966，但在Sechenov数据集上降至0.797-0.893，灵敏度降至0.53-0.67，ECE从0.02升至0.27-0.39，存在显著的泛化差距。级联分类相比单阶段四分类在多数架构上提升了宏F1，但仅对ViT-B/16显著。可调分诊阈值提供了标准单阶段分类无法实现的灵敏度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同深度学习架构和分类方案在皮肤镜图像中的表现，并评估模型从国际公开数据集迁移到俄罗斯独立临床数据集时的泛化能力，以验证模型在实际临床环境中的可靠性。

Method: 采用四种架构（ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S），在三种分类方案（二分类恶性/良性、单阶段四分类良性/MEL/SCC/BCC、两阶段级联先二分类再三分类）下进行实验。所有模型使用ImageNet预训练权重和统一的数据增强方案，在ISIC Archive聚合数据上训练，并在内部保留样本和两个俄罗斯临床数据集上评估。

Result: 内部二阶段ROC-AUC 0.952-0.966；Sechenov数据集降至0.797-0.893，灵敏度降至0.53-0.67，ECE从0.02升至0.27-0.39。配对检验显示ViT-B/16在二阶段存在显著缺陷（p<0.05），分化阶段无架构具显著优势。级联分类提升多数架构宏F1，但仅ViT-B/16显著。ISIC MILK10k上11分类平均灵敏度0.525。

Conclusion: 可调分诊阈值提供了标准单阶段(argmax)分类无法实现的灵敏度控制，更符合临床鉴别诊断逻辑。泛化差距的存在要求模型在部署前必须经过外部临床验证和重新校准。

Abstract: Purpose. To compare deep learning architectures and classification schemes for dermoscopic images of skin neoplasms and assess their generalization on transfer from open international datasets to independent clinical datasets of Russian practice.
  Methods. Four architectures (ViT-B/16, Swin-S, ConvNeXt-S, EfficientNetV2-S) were compared in three schemes: binary (malignant/benign), single-stage four-class (benign, MEL, SCC, BCC), and a two-stage cascade (binary triage, then three-class differentiation MEL/SCC/BCC). All models used ImageNet-pretrained weights and a single augmentation protocol on aggregated open ISIC Archive data, and were evaluated on an internal held-out sample and two clinical datasets (Melanoscope AI mobile system; Sechenov University).
  Results. Internally the binary stage attains ROC-AUC 0.952-0.966; on Sechenov University it drops to 0.797-0.893, sensitivity to 0.53-0.67, and ECE rises from 0.02 to 0.27-0.39 with underestimation of malignancy, quantifying a generalization gap in ranking and calibration. Paired tests confirm one inter-architecture result on clinical data: the deficit of ViT-B/16 at the binary stage (p<0.05); at the differentiation stage no architecture has a proven advantage. The cascade raises macro F1 over single-stage four-class classification for most architectures, but significantly only for ViT-B/16, by recovering malignant lesions assigned to the dominant benign class. On ISIC MILK10k, direct 11-class classification yields mean-class sensitivity 0.525.
  Conclusion. A tunable triage threshold gives sensitivity control not attainable in standard single-stage (argmax) classification and better reproduces clinical differential-diagnosis logic. The persistent generalization gap mandates external clinical validation and recalibration before deployment.

</details>


### [122] [Iterative Visual Thinking: Teaching Vision-Language Models Spatial Self-Correction through Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156)
*Animesh Tripathy,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代视觉思维（IVT）框架，通过两阶段训练（SFT+GRPO）赋予视觉语言模型自我纠错能力，在坐标定位任务上显著提升，仅用2400样本和单GPU即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在单次空间定位上表现良好，但缺乏观察和纠正自身预测的机制。直接提示模型迭代修改会导致性能严重下降（Acc@0.5从79.6%暴跌至48.7%），暴露出定位能力与自我纠错能力之间的根本性差距。

Method: 提出IVT闭环框架：模型预测边界框→在图像上渲染预测结果→通过视觉反馈迭代优化。采用两阶段训练：1) SFT阶段：利用基座模型自身预测作为真实错误样本，提示教师VLM生成纠错推理轨迹，构建无需人工标注的监督数据；2) GRPO阶段：使用IoU奖励进行组相对策略优化，稳定多步细化过程。

Result: 在RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv和Ref-L4混合基准（505个测试样本）上：SFT预热后IVT在各项指标均超单次基座模型，Acc@0.5升至82.0%（+2.4pp），Acc@0.7升至74.1%（+3.2pp），Acc@0.9升至48.3%（+2.8pp）。GRPO将每步IoU退化降低5倍，稳定了细化轨迹。所有训练仅用2400样本和单GPU。

Conclusion: 空间自我纠错是一种可学习的能力，能够以适度的计算成本（2400样本，单GPU）赋予VLM。IVT框架有效弥合了定位能力与自我纠错能力之间的差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong singleshot spatial grounding, yet lack any mechanism to observe and correct their own predictions. We find that naively prompting a VLM to iterate over rendered visualizations of its predictions causes catastrophic failure: Acc@0.5 on referring expression comprehension collapses from 79.6% to 48.7% (a 31 percentage point drop), revealing a fundamental gap between grounding capability and self-correction ability. We propose Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT), a closed-loop framework in which the model predicts a bounding box, observes the prediction rendered on the image, and iteratively refines through visual feedback. A two-phase training recipe closes the self-correction gap: first, we exploit the base model's own predictions as realistic errors and prompt a teacher VLM to generate corrective reasoning traces, yielding supervised data without human annotation; second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a simple IoU reward to stabilize multi-step refinement. On a mixed benchmark spanning RefCOCOg, Ref-Adv, and Ref-L4 (505 test samples), SFT warm-up with IVT surpasses the single-shot base model on every metric: Acc@0.5 rises to 82.0% (+2.4pp), Acc@0.7 to 74.1% (+3.2pp), and Acc@0.9 to 48.3% (+2.8pp). GRPO further reduces per-step IoU degradation by 5x, stabilizing the refinement trajectory. All training uses only 2,400 samples on a single GPU, demonstrating that spatial self-correction is a learnable capability that can be instilled at modest scale.

</details>


### [123] [Transformer-Guided Graph Attention for Direct Cardiac Mesh Reconstruction: A Structural Digital Twin Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13188)
*Abhishek H S,Akash Ganamukhi,Abhimanyu Suresh,Aditya G Hiremath,Prasad B Honnavalli,Adithya Balasubramanyam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出端到端网络，直接从3D医学图像生成平滑、可仿真的心脏表面网格，无需传统分割-网格重建流水线，在MM-WHS 2017基准上取得了有竞争力的分割分数和高质量网格（平均Chamfer距离1.8 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心脏建模流程中网格生成环节（图像分割→Marching Cubes→人工清理）耗时、不一致、需要专业知识，成为临床应用的瓶颈。作者旨在消除这一后处理步骤，使得患者特异性心脏仿真更易用于临床。

Method: 训练单一端到端网络：3D Swin Transformer编码器-解码器从CT/MRI中提取体素特征，配合图注意力网络（GAT）头部迭代变形模板网格以贴合患者心脏边界。一次前向传播即可输出网格，无需Marching Cubes、平滑滤波或人工清理。

Result: 在MM-WHS 2017基准上，CT Dice 0.84，MRI Dice 0.83；平均Chamfer距离1.8 mm，95百分位表面距离低于5 mm。

Conclusion: 对于心脏数字孪生流水线，几何保真度和拓扑正确性比像素级Dice分数更重要。该方法通过移除后处理瓶颈，使患者特异性心脏仿真在临床环境中更具可及性。

Abstract: Building patient-specific cardiac models sits at the heart of precision cardiology, yet getting those models into clinical use keeps running into the same wall: mesh generation is slow, messy, and frustrating. The standard workflow -- segmenting the image, running Marching Cubes, and then manually cleaning up the result -- is time-consuming, inconsistent across operators, and demands specialist knowledge most clinical teams do not have. We take a fundamentally different approach. Instead of treating segmentation and mesh generation as two separate problems, we train a single end-to-end network that goes directly from a raw 3D medical image to a smooth, simulation-ready cardiac surface mesh. The core is a 3D Swin Transformer encoder-decoder that extracts volumetric features from CT or MRI volumes, paired with a Graph Attention Network (GAT) head that iteratively deforms a template mesh to fit the patient's cardiac boundary. We tested on the MM-WHS 2017 benchmark using both CT and MRI. Segmentation scores were competitive (Dice of 0.84 on CT, 0.83 on MRI), but the primary focus is mesh quality: mean Chamfer distance of 1.8 mm, with 95th-percentile surface distance below 5 mm. Every mesh is produced in a single forward pass -- no Marching Cubes, no smoothing filters, no manual cleanup. We argue that for cardiac digital twin pipelines, geometric fidelity and topological correctness matter more than pixel-level Dice scores. By removing the post-processing bottleneck, this approach makes patient-specific cardiac simulation substantially more accessible for clinical use.

</details>


### [124] [Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network for Multimodal CT-PET Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13341)
*Gabriel Steele,Alzahra Altalib,Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域等变生成对抗网络（DDE-GAN），在空间域和频域联合学习，并引入旋转等变约束，用于多模态CT-PET图像合成，在HECKTOR 2022数据集上取得了优于基线模型的合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于GAN的方法仅在空间域操作且忽略几何一致性，导致结构保真度有限，因此需要一种能同时捕捉空间和频谱信息并保持几何等变性的方法。

Method: 在生成器和判别器中联合学习空间域与傅里叶频域特征，将CT和PET测量物理中的旋转等变性嵌入损失函数，并采用分层双域训练策略通过多阶段损失函数强制域内和域间一致性。

Result: 在HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET数据集上，DDE-GAN在CT-PET图像合成质量上显著优于基线模型，合成精度和鲁棒性均有提升。

Conclusion: 双域学习与几何等变性相结合能显著增强多模态图像合成的准确性和鲁棒性，可应用于PET补全和数据增强等实际场景。

Abstract: We present a Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network (DDE-GAN) for multimodal CT-PET image synthesis. Traditional GAN-based approaches often operate solely in the spatial domain and ignore geometric consistency, resulting in limited structural fidelity. DDE-GAN addresses these challenges by jointly learning from both spatial and frequency (Fourier) domains, capturing complementary anatomical and spectral information. Furthermore, rotational equivariance embedded in the physics of the CT and PET measurements are integrated into the loss of both the generator and discriminator to ensure consistent responses under rotations, improving anatomical accuracy. A hierarchical dual-domain training strategy enforces intra- and inter-domain consistency through multi-stage loss functions. Evaluated on the HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET dataset, DDE-GAN achieves superior synthesis quality over baseline models for CT-PET image synthesis. The results demonstrate that combining dual-domain learning with geometric equivariance substantially enhances multimodal image synthesis accuracy and robustness, enabling practical applications in PET completion and data augmentation.

</details>


### [125] [SmartFont: Dynamic Condition Allocation for Few-Shot Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13382)
*Zian Yang,Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartFont是一个基于扩散模型的少样本字体生成框架，通过多层级条件分配机制融合全局内容-风格生成与弱监督局部校正专家，在去噪过程中自适应加权全局内容、全局风格和局部校正特征，实现了更好的全局-局部平衡和字体质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本字体生成方法要么依赖全局内容-风格建模（鲁棒但解耦不完美），要么强调组件/局部建模（捕捉细节但依赖局部先验和参考覆盖）。核心挑战不在于学习更纯净的条件，而在于通过多层级分配组织互补但有偏的全局和局部条件。

Method: 提出SmartFont：1) 扩散基少样本字体生成框架，结合全局内容-风格生成与弱监督局部校正专家；2) 局部分支通过弱组件监督学习专家级局部概念和语义空间图，实现无需显式组件条件推理的细粒度校正；3) 去噪状态条件分配模块自适应地跨时间步和注入块加权全局内容、全局风格和局部校正特征。

Result: 实验表明SmartFont在全局-局部平衡、字形质量和局部细节保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多层级条件分配机制有效组织全局和局部互补条件，而非追求更纯净的解耦，是提升少样本字体生成质量的关键路径。

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Influence Factors on RAG Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12469)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça,Adrien Bécue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过432种配置的全析因实验，系统研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在文档投毒攻击下的鲁棒性，发现检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素，而生成器选择和数据库构成对下游攻击成功率影响最大，且投毒脆弱性源于检索、生成和知识库配置之间的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部检索文档来增强大模型输出，但这种对外部内容的依赖引入了投毒攻击的脆弱性——对抗性文档可能操纵检索过程和生成结果。现有研究缺乏对RAG系统投毒鲁棒性的系统性理解，本文旨在通过大规模实验揭示各组件对投毒脆弱性的影响。

Method: 采用全析因实验设计，覆盖432种配置，系统变化数据集、检索器类型、检索深度、数据库构成、分块策略和生成器模型六个因素，同时在检索层面和生成层面评估投毒攻击的影响。

Result: 检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素；密集检索器和基于图的检索器相比BM25更鲁棒，但更大的检索深度会增加检索到投毒内容的概率；生成器选择和数据库构成对下游攻击成功率影响最大；在多个数据库中复制投毒内容会放大对抗影响，而增加清洁源可缓解这种影响。

Conclusion: RAG的投毒脆弱性并非源于单一组件，而是检索、生成和知识库配置之间交互作用的结果，这为设计和部署更鲁棒的RAG系统提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by grounding responses in retrieved documents from external knowledge sources at inference time. However, this reliance on retrieved content introduces vulnerabilities to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents can manipulate both the retrieval process and the generated outputs. This paper investigates poisoning robustness in RAG through a full factorial experimental study covering 432 configurations. We analyze the impacts of dataset, retriever type, retrieval depth, database composition, chunking strategy, and generator model on retrieval-level and generation-level metrics. The results show that retriever architecture, dataset, and retrieval depth are the strongest factors affecting poisoning exposure, while generator choice and database composition have a major impact on downstream attack success. Dense and graph-based retrievers generally improve robustness relative to BM25, whereas larger retrieval depth increases the likelihood of retrieving poisoned passages. We further show that replicating poisoned content across multiple databases amplifies adversarial influence, while additional clean sources can mitigate it. These findings highlight that poisoning vulnerability in RAG is not attributable to a single component, but instead arises from the interaction of retrieval, generation, and knowledge-base configuration.

</details>


### [127] [From Parameters to Feature Space: Task Arithmetic for Backdoor Mitigation in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12498)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yiya Diao,Weixiang Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出线性特征路径最小化（LFPM）框架，通过引入反后门任务向量在特征空间层面抑制模型合并中的后门攻击，在保持干净任务性能的同时实现鲁棒后门防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并（MM）易受后门攻击，现有基于任务算术的防御方法因依赖直接参数空间编辑，在消除后门时严重损害干净任务性能。

Method: 提出LFPM框架：1）基于跨任务线性（CTL）框架从统一特征空间视角建模后门鲁棒性；2）引入反后门任务向量优化后门抑制；3）采用梯度累积与损失路径积分机制确保沿插值路径的鲁棒后门抑制。

Result: 在全微调与参数高效微调（PEFT）设置下，LFPM均表现出对后门攻击的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LFPM通过特征空间视角的反后门任务向量优化，有效解决了模型合并中防御后门与保持干净性能之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Model merging (MM) has gained significant attention as a cost-effective approach to integrate multiple task-specific models into a unified model. However, recent work reveals that MM is highly susceptible to backdoor attacks. Existing defenses based on task arithmetic often fail to eliminate backdoors without substantially degrading clean-task performance, owing to their reliance on direct parameter-space editing. To address this gap, we propose Linear Feature Path Minimization (LFPM), a backdoor mitigation framework for model merging, which introduces an anti-backdoor task vector into the backdoored merged model. Unlike prior approaches, LFPM formulates the backdoor robustness of the merged model from a unified feature-space perspective under the Cross-Task Linearity (CTL) framework, which leverages the approximate linearity of features across tasks. This perspective guides the optimization of the anti-backdoor task to suppress backdoors while preserving clean-task performance. Furthermore, we introduce an effective optimization mechanism based on gradient accumulation and loss path-integral, ensuring robust backdoor suppression along the interpolation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LFPM consistently exhibits strong robustness against backdoor attacks in both full fine-tuning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) settings.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Examining Trigger Leakage in Vision-Language Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12586)
*Jiamin Chang,Salil Kanhere,Piotr Koniusz,Jason,Xue,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“触发器泄漏”概念，量化视觉后门攻击在非精确触发条件下的误激活问题，并引入邻域泄漏率（NLR）进行度量。实验表明标准微调会导致宽激活区域，通过训练中添加编辑距离为1的硬负样本可有效缩小激活区域、降低泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉后门评估仅关注干净准确率和攻击成功率（ASR），忽略攻击是否具备“精确性”——即仅在预期触发条件下才激活隐藏行为，而实际系统中视觉/语义近似的输入会无意触发恶意行为。

Method: 形式化定义“触发器泄漏”并引入邻域泄漏率（NLR）；使用图标和文本触发器进行实验；通过编辑距离为1的硬负样本训练来缩小激活区域。

Result: 3%投毒率下，图标和文本触发器的邻域变体泄漏严重，NLR分别达0.996和0.944；标准微调学习到宽激活区域；添加硬负样本可显著降低泄漏。

Conclusion: 视觉后门攻击在系统级场景中存在严重的触发器泄漏问题，需采用精确触发条件约束的训练策略来提升攻击的精确性和安全性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Agentic Systems (VLAS) connect visual perception to planning, tool use, and physical actions. This means backdoor-type triggers can propagate through both decision pipelines and their connected interfaces, thus making visual backdoors a system-level threat. Current evaluations on such backdoors focus on clean accuracy and attack success rate (ASR), metrics that capture whether a trigger works, but not whether an attack is actually "precise" -- i.e. whether it triggers hidden behaviors only when intended. In this work, we formalize the failure of trigger precision as "trigger leakage": inputs that are visually or semantically close to the intended trigger and therefore inadvertently activate the attacker-specified behavior. To quantify this leakage, we introduce Neighbor Leakage Rate (NLR). Our experiments show that at a 3% poisoning ratio, icon and text triggers remain robust to common visual transformations, but their neighboring variants leak heavily, with NLR reaching 0.996 (icon) and 0.944 (text). Using textual triggers as a controlled probe, we show that standard fine-tuning learns a broad activation region rather than an exact trigger condition, causing neighboring strings to invoke the malicious behavior even when the exact trigger is absent. Adding edit-distance-one hard-negative samples during training substantially narrows this activation region and reduces leakage, including in image-editing and embodied-manipulation workflows, where leaked triggers can propagate into executable programs and action sequences.

</details>


### [129] [Amnesia: A Stealthy Replay Attack on Continual Learning Dreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12655)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Amnesia攻击，通过仅控制回放索引选择（而非像素/标签/模型参数），在满足可审计约束（如类别分布偏差和回放率）的前提下，利用KL或TV投影优化器，有效降低持续学习模型的准确率并加剧灾难性遗忘，揭示了回放机制中索引控制这一隐蔽且实用的威胁面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习攻击缺乏对回放采样干扰的鲁棒性研究，且多数攻击修改输入或训练流程（投毒/后门），缺少显式的可审计约束，不符合实际场景。本文旨在探索仅控制回放索引选择的有限权限内部攻击者在可审计约束下的威胁能力。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击，两阶段：1) 计算轻量级类别效用（如EMA损失或置信度）以倾斜名义类别分布p0，使其偏向有害类别；2) 利用KL（指数倾斜）或TV（平衡质量重分布）优化器将倾斜投影回可见度预算delta球内。窗口调度器确保滚动审计。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准和强回放基线中，Amnesia持续降低最终准确率并恶化反向迁移。KL变体高影响且难以被多种审计方案检测；TV变体破坏力更强但更易被检测。

Conclusion: 仅索引控制回放是一种实用且可审计的持续学习系统威胁面，建立了原则性的影响-可见度权衡关系。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) models often use experience replay to reduce catastrophic forgetting, but their robustness to replay sampling interference remains underexplored. Existing CL attacks alter inputs or training pipelines (poisoning/backdoors) and rarely include explicit auditable constraints, limiting realism. Here, auditability means a monitor can verify compliance from sampler-visible telemetry - e.g., logged replay index/label statistics - by checking that the realized replay class histogram stays close to a nominal baseline and that replay rate is unchanged per batch and/or over a rolling window. We study a limited-privilege insider who controls only replay index selection, not pixels, labels, or model parameters, while staying within auditable limits such as queue priorities. We introduce Amnesia, a replay composition attack that maximizes degradation under two budgets: a visibility budget delta bounding the TV/KL divergence from a nominal class histogram p0, and a mass budget f fixing the replay rate. Amnesia has two steps: (i) compute lightweight class utilities, such as EMA loss or confidence, to tilt p0 toward harmful classes; and (ii) project the tilt back into the delta-ball using efficient KL (exponential tilt) or TV (balanced mass redistribution) optimizers. A windowed scheduler enforces rolling audits. Across challenging CL benchmarks and strong replay baselines, Amnesia consistently lowers final accuracy (ACC) and worsens backward transfer (-BWT). The KL variant delivers high impact while remaining largely undetected under multiple audit schemes, including per-batch and rolling-window checks. The TV variant is more damaging but easier to detect, especially under tight per-class constraints. These results expose index-only replay control as a practical, auditable threat surface in CL systems and establish a principled impact-visibility trade-off.

</details>


### [130] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPED，一种面向移动GUI代理的上下文感知预上传暴露控制层。它在截图发送至远程多模态代理前，提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析UI元素，仅暴露任务所需内容，屏蔽无关隐私信息。在AndroidWorld评估中，CAPED将加权种子泄露从0.766降至0.268，同时保持较高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于截图的移动GUI代理在执行任务时，每屏截图都可能暴露联系人、消息、照片等与用户请求无关的敏感上下文，导致"偶发视觉隐私暴露"问题。现有文本匿名化和通用隐私屏蔽方法均难以有效应对。

Method: CAPED作为手机端保护层，在截图释放至远程多模态代理前，执行以下步骤：提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析可见UI元素、选择性暴露任务所需内容并屏蔽偶发隐私内容。

Result: 在28任务种子隐私评估中，完整CAPED将成功条件加权种子泄露从原始截图的0.766降至0.268，同时保持高任务效用。AndroidWorld全域评估显示存在原型级效用成本。

Conclusion: 截图上传应被视为明确的设备-云边界决策，由任务驱动的选择性暴露而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来管理。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [131] [SMSR: Certified Defence Against Runtime Memory Poisoning in Persistent LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12703)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统中多会话记忆投毒（MSMP）攻击的防御方法——签名记忆与平滑检索（SMSR），首次为该场景提供了认证鲁棒性保证。SMSR包含两个组件：写入时添加HMAC-SHA256来源认证（阻断未签名注入），查询时采用随机记忆消融与基于判决的多数投票（限制认证攻击者影响）。实验证明该方案能有效将攻击成功率从90%以上降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统支持跨用户会话的持久记忆，这引入了新的攻击面：攻击者可通过正常交互注入恶意记忆，在后续被检索时操控其他用户的响应，而无需修改模型权重或代码。现有防御（如RobustRAG、ReliabilityRAG）仅针对静态语料库，启发式过滤器易被流畅的企业级文本绕过，缺乏针对多会话记忆投毒场景的认证防御。

Method: 提出SMSR防御框架，包含两个组件：（1）写时签名组件：使用HMAC-SHA256对记忆写入操作进行来源认证，阻断未授权注入；（2）查询时平滑检索组件：采用随机记忆消融（randomised memory ablation）结合基于判决的多数投票（verdict-based majority voting），通过形式化推导的超几何分布证书限制认证攻击者的影响。此外，提出了"一致少数效应"（Consistent Minority Effect）理论，分析为何基于字符串的多数投票无法抵御一致性的少数恶意回答，而基于判决的投票可以消除其影响。

Result: 在15个企业场景（3,150次重复试验）中，组件1将未签名变体的攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%。对于单次注入的认证攻击者，组件2将成功率控制在8.0%（95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450），低于认证最坏情况。在端到端纯查询攻击（攻击者诱导代理自身写入投毒）中，SMSR将成功率从65.3%降至5.3%（n=150）。干净查询效用：组件1为90%，组合组件为85%。

Conclusion: SMSR是首个针对多会话记忆投毒（MSMP）提供认证鲁棒性保证的防御方案。证明了无来源认证的查询时过滤器无法对自适应注入提供认证保证。通过签名认证与随机平滑投票的组合，有效解决了RAG系统中持久记忆面临的新型安全威胁，为RAG系统的安全性设计提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) agents increasingly run with persistent memory that accumulates across user sessions. This creates a new attack surface: an adversary interacting only through normal channels can inject crafted memories that, once retrieved, steer the agent's responses for future users, without touching model weights or code. We call this Multi-Session Memory Poisoning (MSMP) and show that no existing defence certifies against it; static-corpus defences (RobustRAG, ReliabilityRAG) assume a fixed knowledge base, and heuristic filters are bypassed by fluent enterprise-style text. We present Signed Memory with Smoothed Retrieval (SMSR), the first defence with a certified robustness bound for this setting. Component 1 adds HMAC-SHA256 provenance at write time, blocking unsigned injection. Component 2 applies randomised memory ablation with verdict-based majority voting at query time, bounding the influence of authenticated adversaries. We prove that no provenance-free retrieval-time filter can certify against adaptive injection, derive a hypergeometric certificate for Component 2, and formalise the Consistent Minority Effect, whereby a consistent adversarial answer wins string-based voting as a numerical minority while verdict-based voting removes it. Across 15 enterprise scenarios (3,150 repeated trials), Component 1 cuts attack success from 93-100% to 0% for all unsigned variants. For an authenticated adversary with a single injection, Component 2 holds success to 8.0% (95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450), below the certified worst case. In an end-to-end query-only attack where the agent itself writes the poison rather than it being pre-seeded, SMSR reduces success from 65.3% to 5.3% (n=150, non-overlapping CIs) on a live agent stack. Clean-query utility is 90% (Component 1) and 85% (combined).

</details>


### [132] [PI-Hunter: Automated Red-Teaming for Exposing and Localizing Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12737)
*Pengfei He,Lesly Miculicich,Vishesh Sharma,Ash Fox,George Lee,Jiliang Tang,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PI-Hunter框架，一种自动化代理审计框架，用于主动暴露LLM代理中的提示注入漏洞。该框架通过构建真实场景测试用例并迭代演化，诱导代理从外部环境检索并暴露潜在恶意指令，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有红队方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要关注推理时阻止恶意内容，红队方法主要优化攻击成功率，导致开发者对潜在提示注入如何在代理中产生和传播的可见性有限。

Method: PI-Hunter构建真实场景的源感知测试用例，并通过反馈驱动的探索迭代演化这些用例，诱导代理从外部环境中检索并暴露潜在恶意指令。

Result: 在多个基准测试、代理架构、攻击和防御下，PI-Hunter在漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖方面显著优于强大的自动化红队基线，且在现有提示注入防御下仍然有效。

Conclusion: PI-Hunter为LLM代理提供了一种有效的自动化审计方法，能够主动暴露提示注入漏洞，帮助开发者获得更全面的安全可见性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into agentic systems that interact with external tools and environments, introducing new security risks such as indirect prompt injection attacks through untrusted external sources. Existing defenses mainly focus on blocking malicious content at inference time, and current red-teaming methods primarily optimize attack success. As a result, developers have limited visibility into how latent prompt injections emerge and propagate through agents. We propose PI-Hunter, an automated agentic auditing framework for proactive vulnerability exposure in LLM agents. PI-Hunter constructs realistic source-aware test cases and iteratively evolves them through feedback-driven exploration to induce agents to retrieve and reveal latent malicious instructions embedded within external environments. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, agent architectures, attacks, and defenses demonstrate that PI-Hunter substantially improves vulnerability exposure and attack-surface coverage over strong automated red-teaming baselines, while remaining effective under existing prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [133] [Semantic Identification of IoT Devices from Behavioral Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12793)
*Samuel Witt,Hassan Habibi Gharakheili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用制造商使用描述（MUD）配置文件进行物联网设备识别，通过ACE级别的语义表示匹配，在运行时变化和稀疏流量条件下相比精确匹配方法表现出更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据包或流记录的物联网设备识别方法依赖底层通信观测，其流量模式会因部署环境、软件版本和用户交互而变化，导致泛化能力不足。本文探索利用MUD配置文件中可解释的ACE级行为语义来提升设备识别的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用28个公开MUD配置文件的1,023个ACE实例，构建ACE级别的语义表示（紧凑行为文本表示），分析其几何特性；评估语义ACE匹配在未见ACE、主机名漂移、部分运行时观测等受控变化下的性能；在包含80万以上真实流量的数据集上验证。

Result: ACE级表示比整体嵌入更能保持设备级别的行为区分性，且经白化校准后依然有效。精确匹配在MUD配置文件重叠度高时表现良好，但重叠稀疏或消失时性能急剧下降；语义匹配在稀疏或零重叠条件下仍能保持有效的识别证据。真实流量中，精确匹配在稳定重叠时最强，但语义匹配在观测初期、候选排序和稀疏重叠场景下更强。

Conclusion: 基于MUD配置文件的ACE语义匹配方法在运行时变化和稀疏流量条件下为物联网设备识别提供了比传统精确匹配更鲁棒的解决方案，尤其在观测初期和重叠稀疏时具有明显优势。

Abstract: Accurate identification of IoT devices is important for security management and policy enforcement. Existing approaches typically learn device signatures from packets or flow records. These methods operate on low-level communication observations whose traffic patterns may vary across deployments, software versions, and user interactions. This paper studies device identification using Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) profiles. MUD profiles describe device behavior using Access Control Entries (ACEs), where each ACE represents a behavioral primitive consisting of protocol, endpoint, direction, and port semantics derived from device communication policy. Our contributions are threefold. First, using 28 publicly available MUD profiles containing 1,023 ACE instances, we construct ACE-level semantic representations from compact behavioral text and analyze their geometric properties. ACE-level representations preserve device-level behavioral distinctions more effectively than whole-profile embeddings and remain effective after whitening calibration. Second, we evaluate semantic ACE matching under controlled runtime variations, including unseen ACEs, drifted hostnames, and partial runtime observation. Exact ACE matching performs well when the overlap with the canonical MUD profile remains high, but degrades sharply when the overlap becomes sparse or disappears. In contrast, semantic ACE matching preserves useful identification evidence across these conditions. Third, we evaluate the same approach on real IoT traffic traces comprising more than 800,000 observed flows. Exact overlap remains the strongest signal when stable overlap exists, while semantic ACE matching provides stronger identification evidence during the early stages of observation, frequently retains the correct device among the highest-ranked candidates, and remains effective under sparse-overlap runtime traffic.

</details>


### [134] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于PySyft平台开发了远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构实现跨机构隐私保护机器学习，用于学生保留率预测。在小型私立大学数据（N=720）上验证了三种合成数据方法，并提出Data-Type-Aware Templates方法。结果表明跨机构分类性能一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），符合FERPA合规要求，为小规模跨机构协作提供了联邦学习的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决教育机构间在保护学生隐私（FERPA合规）的前提下，利用敏感数据进行协作式学生保留率预测的需求，现有联邦学习方案对小型机构而言实施复杂度过高。

Method: 基于PySyft平台构建半气隙架构的远程数据科学框架，包含高侧和低侧服务器；评估三种合成数据生成方法，提出Data-Type-Aware Templates方法，并在三所大学间进行跨机构协作验证。

Result: 跨机构间分类性能稳定（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），严格满足FERPA合规要求，验证了RDS框架在小型教育协作场景中的技术可行性。

Conclusion: 远程数据科学框架下的隐私保护机器学习在教育领域技术上可行，为小规模跨机构协作提供了比联邦学习更实用的替代方案。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [135] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LNTest——首个用于基于闪电网络(LN)的僵尸网络可重复测试平台。通过实验发现：D-LNBot的自组织协议实际生成的是由桥接节点连接的簇状链，而非设计的均匀链；命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)而非此前声称的O(m log n)；不同拓扑结构对拆除策略的抵抗性各异，其中自组织簇状链最为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LNBot和D-LNBot等基于LN的僵尸网络设计仅通过仿真验证，缺乏真实拓扑形成、传播复杂度和抗拆除能力的实证研究。

Method: 构建LNTest测试平台：使用基于Docker容器化的Core Lightning节点和共享的Bitcoin Core regtest链，支持确定性链、自主节点发现和用户提供图三种覆盖拓扑模式。

Result: (1) D-LNBot自组织协议生成的是簇状链，桥接节点移除会导致网络分裂；(2) 命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)，而非O(m log n)；(3) 均匀度链抵抗定向移除但脆弱于随机故障，无标度拓扑相反，自组织簇状链在两种攻击下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest作为开源可重复测试平台，可支持对基于LN的僵尸网络防御的复现研究。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [136] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAStrike是一个面向层次化多智能体系统的闭环红队框架，通过提出首个智能体级Shapley值分析量化各智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献，识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协调性角色感知对抗攻击，在金融、软件工程和CRM等多个领域的前沿模型上显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化多智能体系统在高风险场景中快速部署，但其安全攻击面因角色专业化智能体间的协调对抗行为（如权限提升和跨智能体共谋）而显著扩大。现有红队方法存在局限：依赖启发式选择目标智能体并扰动孤立消息流，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全最负责，以及被攻陷的智能体如何协调绕过防御。

Method: 提出MAStrike闭环框架，包括：(1) 首个智能体级Shapley值分析，量化每个智能体在任务特定分布下对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献；(2) 基于该归因识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协调性角色感知对抗操作；(3) 通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击，将失败案例归因于阻碍对抗尝试的未妥协智能体；(4) 构建涵盖多种层次化拓扑和领域的综合MAS红队基准与可控环境。

Result: 在多个前沿模型构建的MAS上进行的大量实验表明，MAStrike显著优于启发式基线方法。分析还揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构，发现被先前单智能体或模板方法忽视的关键漏洞和协调模式。

Conclusion: MAStrike通过智能体级Shapley值归因和闭环对抗优化，有效解决了层次化MAS中协调性红队攻击的难题，发现了传统方法无法识别的漏洞和智能体间协调模式。
